Found 277 linter issues (4 distinct types) in the Supabase project:

INFO 1: RLS Enabled No Policy (2 issues)
  Description: Detects cases where row level security (RLS) has been enabled on a table but no RLS policies have been created.
  Categories: SECURITY
  How to fix (visit the link to see what to do!): https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/database-linter?lint=0008_rls_enabled_no_policy
  Affected:
  - Table \`public.mfa_sms_codes\` has RLS enabled, but no policies exist
  - Table \`public.social_credentials\` has RLS enabled, but no policies exist

WARN 2: Extension in Public (3 issues)
  Description: Detects extensions installed in the \`public\` schema.
  Categories: SECURITY
  How to fix (visit the link to see what to do!): https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/database-linter?lint=0014_extension_in_public
  Affected:
  - Extension \`citext\` is installed in the public schema. Move it to another schema.
  - Extension \`vector\` is installed in the public schema. Move it to another schema.
  - Extension \`pg_trgm\` is installed in the public schema. Move it to another schema.

WARN 2: Auth RLS Initialization Plan (329 issues)
  Description: Detects if calls to \`current_setting()\` and \`auth.<function>()\` in RLS policies are being unnecessarily re-evaluated for each row
  Categories: PERFORMANCE
  How to fix (visit the link to see what to do!): https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/database-linter?lint=0003_auth_rls_initplan
  Affected:

WARN 3: Multiple Permissive Policies (315 issues)
  Description: Detects if multiple permissive row level security policies are present on a table for the same \`role\` and \`action\` (e.g. insert). Multiple permissive policies are suboptimal for performance as each policy must be executed for every relevant query.
  Categories: PERFORMANCE
  How to fix (visit the link to see what to do!): https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/database-linter?lint=0006_multiple_permissive_policies
  Affected:
